"1818","They are clean mittens.","diliyesulo tsudanvgalvda.","ᏗᎵᏰᏑᎶ ᏧᏓᏅᎦᎸᏓ."
"1819","We are clean kittens, too.","ayv vsquu tsunadanvgalvda wesa anida.","ᎠᏴ ᎥᏍᏊ ᏧᎾᏓᏅᎦᎸᏓ ᏪᏌ ᎠᏂᏓ."
"1820","We like to keep our mittens clean.","tsogatseli diliyesulo dudanvgalvda dogilvquodi.","ᏦᎦᏤᎵ ᏗᎵᏰᏑᎶ ᏚᏓᏅᎦᎸᏓ ᏙᎩᎸᏉᏗ."
"1821","Hear what I say, little kittens.","aquadvnvi itsadvgvga, tsunasdi wesa anida.","ᎠᏆᏛᏅᎢ ᎢᏣᏛᎬᎦ, ᏧᎾᏍᏗ ᏪᏌ ᎠᏂᏓ."
"1822","Try not to soil your mittens.","ditsatseli diliyesulo digadaha nunalisdanelvna itsanetlitanvha.","ᏗᏣᏤᎵ ᏗᎵᏰᏑᎶ ᏗᎦᏓᎭ ᏄᎾᎵᏍᏓᏁᎸᎾ ᎢᏣᏁᏟᏔᏅᎭ."
"1823","""We will try,"" said the kittens.","“dayotsanetlitani,” nuniwesv wesa anida.","“ᏓᏲᏣᏁᏟᏔᏂ,” ᏄᏂᏪᏒ ᏪᏌ ᎠᏂᏓ."
"1824","""We will try to keep them clean.","“vsgina tsudanvgalvda gesesdi dayotsanetlitani”","“ᎥᏍᎩᎾ ᏧᏓᏅᎦᎸᏓ ᎨᏎᏍᏗ ᏓᏲᏣᏁᏟᏔᏂ”"
"1825","Do you soil your mittens, little boy?","unalisdanehos ditsatseli diliyesulo digadaha, usdi atsutsa?","ᎤᎾᎵᏍᏓᏁᎰᏍ ᏗᏣᏤᎵ ᏗᎵᏰᏑᎶ ᏗᎦᏓᎭ, ᎤᏍᏗ ᎠᏧᏣ?"
"1826","Do you soil your mittens, little girl?","unalisdanehos ditsatseli diliyesulo digadaha, usdi ageyutsa?","ᎤᎾᎵᏍᏓᏁᎰᏍ ᏗᏣᏤᎵ ᏗᎵᏰᏑᎶ ᏗᎦᏓᎭ, ᎤᏍᏗ ᎠᎨᏳᏣ?"
"1827","We try to keep our mittens clean.","gotsatseli diliyesulo tsudanvgalvda gesesdi otsanetlidisgo.","ᎪᏣᏤᎵ ᏗᎵᏰᏑᎶ ᏧᏓᏅᎦᎸᏓ ᎨᏎᏍᏗ ᎣᏣᏁᏟᏗᏍᎪ."
"1828","Do we wash our mittens?","dotsvgilosgos tsogatseli diliyesulo?","ᏙᏨᎩᎶᏍᎪᏍ ᏦᎦᏤᎵ ᏗᎵᏰᏑᎶ?"
"1829","Yes, if we soil them.","vv, digadaha gvdayunalisdaneli.","ᎥᎥ, ᏗᎦᏓᎭ ᎬᏓᏳᎾᎵᏍᏓᏁᎵ."
"1830","We shall wash them now.","noquu dodayotsvgiloi.","ᏃᏊ ᏙᏓᏲᏨᎩᎶᎢ."
"1831","We shall wash them clean.","vsgina tsudanvgalvda dodayotsvgiloi.","ᎥᏍᎩᎾ ᏧᏓᏅᎦᎸᏓ ᏙᏓᏲᏨᎩᎶᎢ."
"1832","We shall wash them in the tub.""","svdonihi dodayotsvgiloi.","ᏒᏙᏂᎯ ᏙᏓᏲᏨᎩᎶᎢ."
"1833","Can the kittens wash?","digvwanvgilosdis wesa anida?","ᏗᎬᏩᏅᎩᎶᏍᏗᏍ ᏪᏌ ᎠᏂᏓ?"
"1834","O, yes, they can wash.","o, vv, digvwanvgilosdi.","Ꭳ, ᎥᎥ, ᏗᎬᏩᏅᎩᎶᏍᏗ."
"1835","They can wash their mittens.","tsogatseli diliyesulo digvwanvgilosdi.","ᏦᎦᏤᎵ ᏗᎵᏰᏑᎶ ᏗᎬᏩᏅᎩᎶᏍᏗ."
"1836","They will put them in the tub.","svdonihi dvnilvni.","ᏒᏙᏂᎯ ᏛᏂᎸᏂ."
"1837","They will soap the mittens.","diliyesulohi otla anitlahvsgesdi.","ᏗᎵᏰᏑᎶᎯ ᎣᏝ ᎠᏂᏝᎲᏍᎨᏍᏗ."
"1838","Then they will rub, rub, rub!","nahiyu dodvnasgoliyeiloi!","ᎾᎯᏳ ᏙᏛᎾᏍᎪᎵᏰᎢᎶᎢ!"
"1839","Then they will boil the mittens.","nahiyu diliyesulo dodvnalitlvdani.","ᎾᎯᏳ ᏗᎵᏰᏑᎶ ᏙᏛᎾᎵᏢᏓᏂ."

"1841","They are white and clean.","vsgina tsunega ale tsudanvgalvda.","ᎥᏍᎩᎾ ᏧᏁᎦ ᎠᎴ ᏧᏓᏅᎦᎸᏓ."
"1842","Rub, rub, rub!","gegasgoliyeilovi!","ᎨᎦᏍᎪᎵᏰᎢᎶᎥᎢ!"
"1843","Now the mittens are clean.","noquu diliyesulo tsudanvgalvda.","ᏃᏊ ᏗᎵᏰᏑᎶ ᏧᏓᏅᎦᎸᏓ."
"1844","Boil, boil, boil!","gegalitlvhilovi!","ᎨᎦᎵᏢᎯᎶᎥᎢ!"
"1845","Now the mittens are white.","noquu diliyesulo tsunega.","ᏃᏊ ᏗᎵᏰᏑᎶ ᏧᏁᎦ."
"1846","What will the kittens do now?","gado noquu dvnadvneli wesa anida?","ᎦᏙ ᏃᏊ ᏛᎾᏛᏁᎵ ᏪᏌ ᎠᏂᏓ?"
"1847","They will dry the mittens.","diliyesulo dodvnikayotani.","ᏗᎵᏰᏑᎶ ᏙᏛᏂᎧᏲᏔᏂ."
"1848","They will hang them up.","dodvnadvni.","ᏙᏛᎾᏛᏂ."
"1849","They will hang them up to dry.","unikayohisdi dodvnadvni.","ᎤᏂᎧᏲᎯᏍᏗ ᏙᏛᎾᏛᏂ."
"1850","They will hang them out in the sun.","agalisgv dodvnadvni.","ᎠᎦᎵᏍᎬ ᏙᏛᎾᏛᏂ."
"1851","See, the mittens are on the line.","ni, diliyesulo sdeyida anadaa.","Ꮒ, ᏗᎵᏰᏑᎶ ᏍᏕᏱᏓ ᎠᎾᏓᎠ."
"1852","Now they will dry.","noquu dvnikayosi.","ᏃᏊ ᏛᏂᎧᏲᏏ."
"1853","They will hang in the sun.","agalisgv anadaesdi.","ᎠᎦᎵᏍᎬ ᎠᎾᏓᎡᏍᏗ."
"1854","They will hang on the line.","sdeyidai anadaesdi.","ᏍᏕᏱᏓᎢ ᎠᎾᏓᎡᏍᏗ."

"1856","They will dry on the line.","sdeyidai dvnikayosi.","ᏍᏕᏱᏓᎢ ᏛᏂᎧᏲᏏ."
"1857","Here is a magpie.","ani watiyeli.","ᎠᏂ ᏩᏘᏰᎵ."
"1858","The magpie is a bird.","watiyeli tsisqua.","ᏩᏘᏰᎵ ᏥᏍᏆ."
"1859","The magpie can talk.","watiyeli eliquu yigawoniha.","ᏩᏘᏰᎵ ᎡᎵᏊ ᏱᎦᏬᏂᎭ."
"1860","The magpie can steal.","watiyeli eliquu yiganosga.","ᏩᏘᏰᎵ ᎡᎵᏊ ᏱᎦᏃᏍᎦ."
"1861","The magpie likes to talk.","watiyeli uwonihisdi ulvquodi.","ᏩᏘᏰᎵ ᎤᏬᏂᎯᏍᏗ ᎤᎸᏉᏗ."
"1862","The magpie likes to steal.","watiyeli unosgisdi ulvquodi.","ᏩᏘᏰᎵ ᎤᏃᏍᎩᏍᏗ ᎤᎸᏉᏗ."
"1863","The magpie saw the kittens.","watiyeli wesa anida dugohe.","ᏩᏘᏰᎵ ᏪᏌ ᎠᏂᏓ ᏚᎪᎮ."
"1864","The magpie saw the mittens.","watiyeli diliyesulo dugohe.","ᏩᏘᏰᎵ ᏗᎵᏰᏑᎶ ᏚᎪᎮ."

"1874","""How do you do, kittens?""","“osiquutsu itsiwesa itsida?”","“ᎣᏏᏊᏧ ᎢᏥᏪᏌ ᎢᏥᏓ?”"

"1876","Do you see our white mittens?""","dehigowatihas tsogatseli tsunega diliyesulo?”","ᏕᎯᎪᏩᏘᎭᏍ ᏦᎦᏤᎵ ᏧᏁᎦ ᏗᎵᏰᏑᎶ?”"
"1877","""Yes, I see them,"" said the magpie.","“vv, detsigowatiha,” udvne watiyeli.","“ᎥᎥ, ᏕᏥᎪᏩᏘᎭ,” ᎤᏛᏁ ᏩᏘᏰᎵ."

"1885","O, just for fun,"" said the magpie.","“o, galieligaquu,"" nuwese watiyeli.","“Ꭳ, ᎦᎵᎡᎵᎦᏊ,"" ᏄᏪᏎ ᏩᏘᏰᎵ."

"1888","""What a strange bird!"" said the kittens.","“tsisqua uwolidi!” nuniwese wesa anida.","“ᏥᏍᏆ ᎤᏬᎵᏗ!” ᏄᏂᏪᏎ ᏪᏌ ᎠᏂᏓ."

"2403","Where are you going, Cocky Locky ?","hatlv hega tsvtsvya atsvya?","ᎭᏢ ᎮᎦ ᏨᏨᏯ ᎠᏨᏯ?"
"2404","Where are you going, Ducky Lucky?","hatlv hega sawonu kawonu?","ᎭᏢ ᎮᎦ ᏌᏬᏄ ᎧᏬᏄ?"
"2405","Where are you going, Goosey Loosey?","hatlv hega sisi sasa?","ᎭᏢ ᎮᎦ ᏏᏏ ᏌᏌ?"
"2406","Where are you going?"" said Turkey Lurkey.","hatlv itsega?” nuwese gvsa gvna.","ᎭᏢ ᎢᏤᎦ?” ᏄᏪᏎ ᎬᏌ ᎬᎾ."
"2407","Then those six and a hungry fox met.","nahiyu na sudali ale uyosisgvi tsutli danatlose.","ᎾᎯᏳ Ꮎ ᏑᏓᎵ ᎠᎴ ᎤᏲᏏᏍᎬᎢ ᏧᏟ ᏓᎾᏠᏎ."
"2408","“Fox fox” was his name.","“tsula tsutli” dudov tsutli.","“ᏧᎳ ᏧᏟ” ᏚᏙᎥ ᏧᏟ."
"2409","“Where are you going?” said Foxy Loxy.","“hatlv itsega?” nuwese tsula tsutli.","“ᎭᏢ ᎢᏤᎦ?” ᏄᏪᏎ ᏧᎳ ᏧᏟ."
"2410","""Why, why, why!"" said Foxy Loxy.","“gado, gado, gado!” nuwese tsula tsutli.","“ᎦᏙ, ᎦᏙ, ᎦᏙ!” ᏄᏪᏎ ᏧᎳ ᏧᏟ."
"2411","And the tree told us.""","tlugvno oginohetlelv.","ᏡᎬᏃ ᎣᎩᏃᎮᏞᎸ."
"2412","""I will go with you, if I may,"" said Foxy Loxy.","“dedaligonvha ale idenvsvha,” nuwese tsula tsutli.","“ᏕᏓᎵᎪᏅᎭ ᎠᎴ ᎢᏕᏅᏒᎭ,” ᏄᏪᏎ ᏧᎳ ᏧᏟ."
"2413","And away the seven ran.","ale na galiquogi unalitise.","ᎠᎴ Ꮎ ᎦᎵᏉᎩ ᎤᎾᎵᏘᏎ."
"2414","""Come with me,"" said the fox.","“idaligohvga ale idena,” nuwese tsula tsutli.","“ᎢᏓᎵᎪᎲᎦ ᎠᎴ ᎢᏕᎾ,” ᏄᏪᏎ ᏧᎳ ᏧᏟ."
"2415","""I will show you the way.""","“nusdi datsvgowadvtani.”","“ᏄᏍᏗ ᏓᏨᎪᏩᏛᏔᏂ.”"
"2416","""Yes,"" said Chicken Little.","“vv,” nuwese tsitaga usdi.","“ᎥᎥ,” ᏄᏪᏎ ᏥᏔᎦ ᎤᏍᏗ."
"2417","""Yes,"" said Henny Penny.","“vv,” nuwese sigisi agisi.","“ᎥᎥ,” ᏄᏪᏎ ᏏᎩᏏ ᎠᎩᏏ."
"2418","""Yes,"" said Cocky Locky.","“vv,” nuwese tsvtsvya atsvya.","“ᎥᎥ,” ᏄᏪᏎ ᏨᏨᏯ ᎠᏨᏯ."
"2419","""Yes,"" said Ducky Lucky.","“vv,” nuwese sawonu kawonu.","“ᎥᎥ,” ᏄᏪᏎ ᏌᏬᏄ ᎧᏬᏄ."
"2420","""Yes,"" said Goosey Loosey.","“vv,” nuwese sisi sasa.","“ᎥᎥ,” ᏄᏪᏎ ᏏᏏ ᏌᏌ."
"2421","""Yes,"" said Turkey Lurkey.","“vv,” nuwese gvsa gvna.","“ᎥᎥ,” ᏄᏪᏎ ᎬᏌ ᎬᎾ."
"2422","""We will go with you.","“dadaligoni ale dadenvsi.","“ᏓᏓᎵᎪᏂ ᎠᎴ ᏓᏕᏅᏏ."
"2423","Show us the way.""","nusdi isgigowadvda.”","ᏄᏍᏗ ᎢᏍᎩᎪᏩᏛᏓ.”"
"2424","So Foxy Loxy led Chicken Little, Henny Penny, Cocky Locky, Ducky Lucky, Groosey Loosey and Turkey Lurkey into the woods.","nole tsula tsutli vsgina dutinvse inagei ayeli, tsitaga usdi, sigisi agisi, tsvtsvya atsvya, sawonu kawonu, sisi sasa, ale gvsa gvna.","ᏃᎴ ᏧᎳ ᏧᏟ ᎥᏍᎩᎾ ᏚᏘᏅᏎ ᎢᎾᎨᎢ ᎠᏰᎵ, ᏥᏔᎦ ᎤᏍᏗ, ᏏᎩᏏ ᎠᎩᏏ, ᏨᏨᏯ ᎠᏨᏯ, ᏌᏬᏄ ᎧᏬᏄ, ᏏᏏ ᏌᏌ, ᎠᎴ ᎬᏌ ᎬᎾ."
"2425","Foxy Loxy had a cave in the woods.","tsula tsuli utseli usdagalvi inagei ayeli.","ᏧᎳ ᏧᎵ ᎤᏤᎵ ᎤᏍᏓᎦᎸᎢ ᎢᎾᎨᎢ ᎠᏰᎵ."
"2426","The cave was his home.","na usdagalv tsuwenvsvi.","Ꮎ ᎤᏍᏓᎦᎸ ᏧᏪᏅᏒᎢ."
"2427","So he led Chicken Little and her friends to the cave.","usdagalvi dutinvse, tsitaga usdi ale tsunali.","ᎤᏍᏓᎦᎸᎢ ᏚᏘᏅᏎ, ᏥᏔᎦ ᎤᏍᏗ ᎠᎴ ᏧᎾᎵ."
"2428","""O! O! O!"" they cried.","“o! o! o!” sdaya nuniwese.","“Ꭳ! Ꭳ! Ꭳ!” ᏍᏓᏯ ᏄᏂᏪᏎ."
"2429","""Where is the king?""","“hatlv ugvwiyuhi?”","“ᎭᏢ ᎤᎬᏫᏳᎯ?”"
"2430","The hungry fox laughed.","na uyosigvi tsutli uyetise.","Ꮎ ᎤᏲᏏᎬᎢ ᏧᏟ ᎤᏰᏘᏎ."
"2431","Alas, poor silly Chicken Little!","o, uyo uwolidi tsitaga usdi!","Ꭳ, ᎤᏲ ᎤᏬᎵᏗ ᏥᏔᎦ ᎤᏍᏗ!"
"2432","Alas, poor silly Henny Penny!","o, uyo uwolidi sigisi agisi!","Ꭳ, ᎤᏲ ᎤᏬᎵᏗ ᏏᎩᏏ ᎠᎩᏏ!"
"2433","Alas, poor silly Cocky Locky!","o, uyo uwolidi tsvtsvya atsvya!","Ꭳ, ᎤᏲ ᎤᏬᎵᏗ ᏨᏨᏯ ᎠᏨᏯ!"
"2434","Alas, poor silly Ducky Lucky!","o, uyo uwolidi sawonu kawonu!","Ꭳ, ᎤᏲ ᎤᏬᎵᏗ ᏌᏬᏄ ᎧᏬᏄ!"
"2435","Alas, poor silly Goosey Loosey!","o, uyo uwolidi sisi sasa!","Ꭳ, ᎤᏲ ᎤᏬᎵᏗ ᏏᏏ ᏌᏌ!"
"2436","Alas, poor silly Turkey Lurkey!","o, uyo uwolidi gvsa gvna!","Ꭳ, ᎤᏲ ᎤᏬᎵᏗ ᎬᏌ ᎬᎾ!"
"2437","And the bad fox laughed and laughed.","ale uyoi tsutli uyetisiloe.","ᎠᎴ ᎤᏲᎢ ᏧᏟ ᎤᏰᏘᏏᎶᎡ."
